    Theme = pages.UI
    do
        local function hx(c)
            return string.format("#%02X%02X%02X", math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5))
        end
        heading(Theme, "ACCENT COLOR", 1)
        local preview = Instance.new("Frame")
        preview.Name = "AccentPreview"
        preview.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.slab
        preview.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        preview.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 52)
        preview.LayoutOrder = 2
        preview.BorderSizePixel = 0
        preview.Parent = Theme
        round(preview, 7)
        edge(preview, SKIN.seamSoft, 1, 0.45)
        local swatch = Instance.new("Frame")
        swatch.Name = "Swatch"
        swatch.Size = UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, 36)
        swatch.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9, 0.5, -18)
        swatch.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT
        swatch.BorderSizePixel = 0
        swatch.ZIndex = 5
        swatch.Parent = preview
        round(swatch, 6)
        edge(swatch, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1, 0.6)
        local title = caption(preview, "CURRENT ACCENT", 11, TYPE_HEAVY, SKIN.ink)
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 10)
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 16)
        title.ZIndex = 5
        local hexLbl = caption(preview, hx(ACCENT), 10, TYPE_BOLD, SKIN.inkMute)
        hexLbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 55, 0, 27)
        hexLbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 14)
        hexLbl.ZIndex = 5
        heading(Theme, "PRESETS", 3)
        local grid = Instance.new("Frame")
        grid.Name = "Swatches"
        grid.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        grid.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 68)
        grid.LayoutOrder = 4
        grid.Parent = Theme
        local gl = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        gl.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
        gl.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
        gl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        gl.HorizontalAlignment = Enum.HorizontalAlignment.Center
        gl.Parent = grid
        local presets = {
            Color3.fromRGB(56, 138, 255), Color3.fromRGB(88, 166, 255), Color3.fromRGB(120, 190, 255),
            Color3.fromRGB(150, 212, 255), Color3.fromRGB(40, 108, 226), Color3.fromRGB(28, 84, 190),
            Color3.fromRGB(70, 200, 255), Color3.fromRGB(46, 170, 230), Color3.fromRGB(96, 132, 235),
            Color3.fromRGB(132, 148, 255), Color3.fromRGB(176, 206, 245), Color3.fromRGB(212, 232, 255),
        }
        for idx, col in ipairs(presets) do
            local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
            b.Name = "SW" .. idx
            b.Text = ""
            b.AutoButtonColor = false
            b.BackgroundColor3 = col
            b.BorderSizePixel = 0
            b.LayoutOrder = idx
            b.Parent = grid
            round(b, 6)
            edge(b, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 1, 0.65)
            b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() applyAccent(col) end)
        end
        heading(Theme, "HUE", 5)
        local hueRow = Instance.new("Frame")
        hueRow.Name = "HueSlider"
        hueRow.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.slab
        hueRow.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        hueRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 44)
        hueRow.LayoutOrder = 6
        hueRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
        hueRow.Parent = Theme
        round(hueRow, 7)
        edge(hueRow, SKIN.seamSoft, 1, 0.45)
        local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
        bar.Name = "Bar"
        bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 10)
        bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0.5, -5)
        bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
        bar.Parent = hueRow
        round(bar, 5)
        local hg = Instance.new("UIGradient")
        hg.Color = ColorSequence.new({
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.17, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.33, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 0)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.50, Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.67, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0.83, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 255)),
            ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1.00, Color3.fromRGB(255, 0, 0)),
        })
        hg.Parent = bar
        local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
        knob.Name = "Knob"
        knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
        knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
        knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
        knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
        knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
        knob.ZIndex = 3
        knob.Parent = bar
        round(knob, 7)
        edge(knob, SKIN.void, 2, 0)
        local HUE_SAT, HUE_VAL = 0.72, 1.0
        local dragging = false
        local function setKnobFromColor(c)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(select(1, c:ToHSV()), 0, 0.5, 0)
        end
        local function updateFromX(px)
            local absX = bar.AbsolutePosition.X
            local absW = bar.AbsoluteSize.X
            if absW <= 0 then return end
            local frac = math.clamp((px - absX) / absW, 0, 1)
            knob.Position = UDim2.new(frac, 0, 0.5, 0)
            applyAccent(Color3.fromHSV(frac, HUE_SAT, HUE_VAL))
        end
        local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
        hit.Name = "Hit"
        hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        hit.Text = ""
        hit.AutoButtonColor = false
        hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 12, 3, 0)
        hit.Position = UDim2.new(0, -6, -1, 0)
        hit.ZIndex = 4
        hit.Parent = bar
        hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
                dragging = true
                updateFromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(i)
            if dragging and (i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
                updateFromX(i.Position.X)
            end
        end)
        UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(i)
            if i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or i.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then dragging = false end
        end)
        pushRow(Theme, "Reset Accent", "RESET", 7, function()
            applyAccent(DEFAULT_ACCENT)
            setKnobFromColor(DEFAULT_ACCENT)
        end)
        setKnobFromColor(ACCENT)
        registerAccent(function(c)
            swatch.BackgroundColor3 = c
            hexLbl.Text = hx(c)
            setKnobFromColor(c)
        end)
        heading(Theme, "BACKGROUND", 8)
        local deck = Instance.new("Frame")
        deck.Name = "BackdropDeck"
        deck.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.slab
        deck.BackgroundTransparency = 0.25
        deck.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 108)
        deck.BorderSizePixel = 0
        deck.LayoutOrder = 9
        deck.ZIndex = 4
        deck.Parent = Theme
        round(deck, 7)
        edge(deck, SKIN.seamSoft, 1, 0.45)
        inset(deck, 8, 8, 8, 8)
        local deckGrid = Instance.new("UIGridLayout")
        deckGrid.CellSize = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 42)
        deckGrid.CellPadding = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6)
        deckGrid.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
        deckGrid.Parent = deck
        local deckButtons = {}
        function updateBackgroundButtons()
            for index, holder in pairs(deckButtons) do
                local on = index == currentBackground
                local st = holder:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
                if st then
                    st.Color = on and ACCENT or SKIN.seamSoft
                    glide(st, {Transparency = on and 0.05 or 0.55, Thickness = on and 1.4 or 1})
                end
                glide(holder, {BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 0.35})
            end
        end
        local function deckTile(index, name, order)
            local style = BACKDROPS[index]
            local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
            holder.Name = "BD" .. tostring(index)
            holder.BackgroundColor3 = style and (style.accent and accentDim(ACCENT, 0.45) or style.color) or SKIN.void
            holder.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
            holder.BorderSizePixel = 0
            holder.LayoutOrder = order
            holder.ZIndex = 6
            holder.ClipsDescendants = true
            holder.Parent = deck
            round(holder, 6)
            edge(holder, SKIN.seamSoft, 1, 0.55)
            if style and style.image and style.image ~= "rbxassetid://0" then
                local art = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
                art.BackgroundTransparency = 1
                art.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
                art.Image = style.image
                art.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
                art.ImageTransparency = 0.3
                art.ZIndex = 6
                art.Parent = holder
            end
            local cap = caption(holder, name, 9, TYPE_HEAVY, SKIN.ink, Enum.TextXAlignment.Center)
            cap.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            cap.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
            cap.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.4
            cap.ZIndex = 7
            local click = Instance.new("TextButton")
            click.BackgroundTransparency = 1
            click.Text = ""
            click.AutoButtonColor = false
            click.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
            click.ZIndex = 8
            click.Parent = holder
            deckButtons[index] = holder
            if style and style.accent then
                registerAccent(function() holder.BackgroundColor3 = accentDim(ACCENT, 0.45) end)
            end
            click.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                applyBackground(index)
                updateBackgroundButtons()
            end)
        end
        deckTile(0, "OFF", 1)
        for i, style in ipairs(BACKDROPS) do
            deckTile(i, string.upper(style.name), i + 1)
        end
        updateBackgroundButtons()
        registerAccent(updateBackgroundButtons)
    end
    task.wait()
    System = pages.SETTINGS
    waterGuiScaleValue = math.clamp(tonumber(savedConfig.waterGuiScaleValue) or tonumber(waterGuiScaleValue) or 0.72, 0.50, 1.50)
    waterProgressBarScaleValue = tonumber(savedConfig.waterProgressBarScaleValue) or waterProgressBarScaleValue
    waterMainScale = Main:FindFirstChild("WaterHubMainScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
    waterMainScale.Name = "WaterHubMainScale"
    waterMainScale.Scale = waterGuiScaleValue
    waterMainScale.Parent = Main
    function applyWaterHubProgressBarScale()
        local sg = PlayerGui:FindFirstChild("StealBarGui")
        local bar = sg and sg:FindFirstChild("StealBar")
        if not bar then return end
        local sc = bar:FindFirstChild("WaterHubProgressBarScale") or Instance.new("UIScale")
        sc.Name = "WaterHubProgressBarScale"
        sc.Scale = waterProgressBarScaleValue
        sc.Parent = bar
    end
    heading(System, "PANEL", 1)
    do
        local row, setVisual, hit = switchRow(System, "Lock GUI", _G.WaterHubGuiLocked == true, 2)
        setLockGuiVisual = setVisual
        hit.Activated:Connect(function()
            _G.WaterHubGuiLocked = not (_G.WaterHubGuiLocked == true)
            setVisual(_G.WaterHubGuiLocked == true)
            if WaterHubUpdateGuiLockVisual then WaterHubUpdateGuiLockVisual() end
            saveWaterHubConfig()
        end)
    end
    stepperRow(System, "GUI Scale", waterGuiScaleValue, 3, function(v)
        waterGuiScaleValue = v
        waterMainScale.Scale = v
        saveWaterHubConfig()
    end)
    stepperRow(System, "Progress Bar Size", waterProgressBarScaleValue, 4, function(v)
        waterProgressBarScaleValue = v
        applyWaterHubProgressBarScale()
        saveWaterHubConfig()
    end)
    bindRow(System, "Toggle UI", "ToggleUI", 5)
    heading(System, "MOBILE BUTTONS", 6)
    do
        local row, setVisual, hit = switchRow(System, "Hide Mobile Buttons", _G.WaterHubHideMobileButtons == true, 7)
        setHideMobileButtonsVisual = setVisual
        hit.Activated:Connect(function()
            _G.WaterHubHideMobileButtons = not (_G.WaterHubHideMobileButtons == true)
            setVisual(_G.WaterHubHideMobileButtons == true)
            if _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
            saveWaterHubConfig()
        end)
    end
    stepperRow(System, "Mobile Buttons Size", tonumber(_G.WaterHubMobileButtonScale) or 0.75, 8, function(v)
        _G.WaterHubMobileButtonScale = math.clamp(tonumber(v) or 0.35, 0.30, 1.35)
        if _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonSize then _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonSize() end
        saveWaterHubConfig()
    end, 0.30, 1.35)
    pushRow(System, "Reset Mobile Buttons", "RESET", 9, function()
        if _G.WaterHubResetMobileButtons then
            _G.WaterHubResetMobileButtons()
        else
            _G.WaterHubMobileButtonScale = 0.75
            _G.WaterHubHideMobileButtons = false
            if _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
            if _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonSize then _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonSize() end
            saveWaterHubConfig()
        end
    end)
    heading(System, "CONFIG", 10)
    pushRow(System, "Import Config", "OPEN", 11, function()
        if _G.ImportPanelToggle then _G.ImportPanelToggle() end
    end)
    heading(System, "SETTINGS", 900)
    do
        local holder = Instance.new("Frame")
        holder.Name = "ResetHolder"
        holder.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        holder.Size = UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 44)
        holder.LayoutOrder = 901
        holder.ZIndex = 5
        holder.Parent = System
        local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
        btn.Name = "ResetAll"
        btn.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.well
        btn.BackgroundTransparency = 0
        btn.BorderSizePixel = 0
        btn.Text = "RESET ALL SETTINGS"
        btn.TextColor3 = SKIN.ink
        btn.TextSize = 11
        btn.Font = TYPE_HEAVY
        btn.AutoButtonColor = false
        btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 40)
        btn.ZIndex = 6
        btn.Parent = holder
        round(btn, 7)
        local st = edge(btn, Color3.fromRGB(212, 74, 74), 1.2, 0.25)
        local armed = false
        local timer = nil
        local function idleTheme()
            armed = false
            btn.Text = "RESET ALL SETTINGS"
            btn.TextColor3 = SKIN.ink
            glide(btn, {BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.well})
            st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(212, 74, 74)
            glide(st, {Transparency = 0.25, Thickness = 1.2})
        end
        local function armedTheme()
            btn.Text = "CLICK AGAIN TO CONFIRM"
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 206, 92)
            glide(btn, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(38, 30, 18)})
            st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 206, 92)
            glide(st, {Transparency = 0.05, Thickness = 1.5})
        end
        local function doneTheme()
            btn.Text = "DONE \226\128\148 REJOINING"
            btn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(140, 230, 160)
            glide(btn, {BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(20, 38, 26)})
            st.Color = Color3.fromRGB(140, 230, 160)
            glide(st, {Transparency = 0.05, Thickness = 1.5})
        end
        btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if not armed then
                armed = true
                armedTheme()
                if timer then task.cancel(timer) end
                timer = task.delay(3, idleTheme)
                return
            end
            if timer then
                task.cancel(timer)
                timer = nil
            end
            btn.Text = "RESETTING\226\128\166"
            pcall(function()
                local files = {
                    CONFIG_FILE,
                    KEYBINDS_CONFIG_FILE,
                    "WaterHub_MainGUI_Config.json",
                    "WaterHubConfig.json",
                    "WaterHub_Settings.json",
                    "WaterHub_Keybinds.json",
                    "WaterHub_GUI.json",
                }
                for _, fname in ipairs(files) do
                    pcall(function()
                        if fname and isfile and isfile(fname) and delfile then delfile(fname) end
                    end)
                end
            end)
            pcall(function()
                waterGuiScaleValue = 0.72
                waterProgressBarScaleValue = 0.83
                NS = 59.5; CS = 28.8; LAGGER_SPEED = 29; LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = 15
                currentSpeedMode = "Normal"
                autoCarrySpeedEnabled = false
                autoTPHeight = 20
                autoStealEnabled = false; selectedStealMode = "Semi"; autoStealRadius = 9
                _G.WaterHubStealRadii = {Normal = 9, Semi = 9}
                selectedAnimationPack = "OFF"
                AIMBOT_SPEED = 58; LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = 40; TPBAT_SPEED = 58
                autoSwingEnabled = false; mirrorTPDownEnabled = false; tpBatSwingEnabled = false
                _G.AimbotOn = false; _G.TPBatOn = false
                antiRagdollEnabled = false; infJumpEnabled = false; autoTPEnabled = false
                batCounterEnabled = false; medCounterEnabled = false; safeModeEnabled = false; autoResetOnMedEnabled = false
                if _G.WaterHubSetAntiDie then _G.WaterHubSetAntiDie(false, true) end
                if _G.WaterHubSetInstaResetOnDeath then _G.WaterHubSetInstaResetOnDeath(false, true) else autoInstaResetOnDeathEnabled = false end
                pcall(function() applyAccent(DEFAULT_ACCENT, true) end)
                espEnabled = false; showTracerEnabled = false; ragdollCountdownEnabled = false
                stretchRezEnabled = false; antiLagEnabled = false; nukeOptimiserEnabled = false
                fovEnabled = false; fovValue = 70; noCamCollisionEnabled = false; _G.WaterHubNoPlayerCollisionEnabled = false
                skyTheme = "Off"; currentBackground = 0
                autoLeftEnabled = false; autoRightEnabled = false
                _G.WaterHubGuiLocked = false; _G.WaterHubHideMobileButtons = false; _G.WaterHubMobileButtonScale = 0.75
                waterMainScale.Scale = waterGuiScaleValue
                applyWaterHubProgressBarScale()
                applyBackground(0)
                updateBackgroundButtons()
                applyDefaultWaterHubKeybinds()
                refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
                refreshTPDownKeybind()
                if stopAutoTP then stopAutoTP() end
                if stopAntiRagdoll then stopAntiRagdoll() end
                if normalSpeedBox then normalSpeedBox.Text = tostring(NS) end
                if carrySpeedBox then carrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(CS) end
                if laggerSpeedBox then laggerSpeedBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED) end
                if laggerCarrySpeedBox then laggerCarrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED) end
                if autoTPHeightBox then autoTPHeightBox.Text = tostring(autoTPHeight) end
                if _G.WaterHubSetGrabRadius then _G.WaterHubSetGrabRadius(tostring(autoStealRadius)) end
                if _G.AimbotSyncSpeedBoxes then _G.AimbotSyncSpeedBoxes() end
                if type(applyCustomSky) == "function" then applyCustomSky("Off") end
                if skyValueLabel then skyValueLabel.Text = "OFF" end
                if _G.WaterHubResetMobileButtons then _G.WaterHubResetMobileButtons() end
                if _G.WaterHubApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.WaterHubApplySavedGameplayStates() end
                saveWaterHubConfig()
            end)
            task.wait(0.35)
            doneTheme()
            task.wait(0.6)
            pcall(function()
                local TeleportService = game:GetService("TeleportService")
                TeleportService:Teleport(game.PlaceId, Players.LocalPlayer)
            end)
        end)
        idleTheme()
    end
    do
        local lastTPBat, lastLock, lastMode = nil, nil, nil
        RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
            local tpBat = _G.TPBatOn == true
            if tpBat ~= lastTPBat then
                lastTPBat = tpBat
                if _G.TPBatSetVisual then _G.TPBatSetVisual(tpBat) end
            end
            local lock = _G.AimbotOn == true
            if lock ~= lastLock then
                lastLock = lock
                if _G.AimbotSyncVisual then _G.AimbotSyncVisual() end
            end
            if currentSpeedMode ~= lastMode then
                lastMode = currentSpeedMode
                refreshSpeedModeRows()
            end
        end)
    end
    _G.WaterHubSyncToggleVisuals = function()
        pcall(function() if setAutoStealVisual then setAutoStealVisual(autoStealEnabled == true) end end)
        pcall(function() if setInfJumpVisual then setInfJumpVisual(infJumpEnabled == true) end end)
        pcall(function() if setAntiRagdollVisual then setAntiRagdollVisual(antiRagdollEnabled == true) end end)
        pcall(function() if setAutoCarrySpeedVisual then setAutoCarrySpeedVisual(autoCarrySpeedEnabled == true) end end)
        pcall(function() if setAutoTPVisual then setAutoTPVisual(autoTPEnabled == true) end end)
        pcall(function() if setAutoResetOnMedVisual then setAutoResetOnMedVisual(autoResetOnMedEnabled == true) end end)
        pcall(function() if setAntiDieVisual then setAntiDieVisual(antiDieEnabled == true) end end)

        pcall(function() if _G.WaterHubSetAutoRightVisual then _G.WaterHubSetAutoRightVisual(autoRightEnabled == true) end end)
    end
    if WaterHubUpdateGuiLockVisual then WaterHubUpdateGuiLockVisual() end
    if _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonsHidden then _G.WaterHubApplyMobileButtonsHidden() end
end
do
    UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gameProcessed)
        local isControllerInput = tostring(input.UserInputType):find("Gamepad") ~= nil
        if gameProcessed and input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not listeningForSpeedKey and not listeningForTPDownKey then return end
        if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
        if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard and not isControllerInput then return end
        if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
        if speedKeybinds.ToggleUI and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.ToggleUI then
            if Main.Visible then
                Main.Visible = false
                MiniFrame.Visible = true
            else
                Main.Visible = true
                MiniFrame.Visible = false
                Main.Size = FULL_MAIN_SIZE
            end
            saveWaterHubConfig()
            return
        end
        if listeningForSpeedKey then
            if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
            local targetKey = listeningForSpeedKey
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                listeningForSpeedKey = nil
                refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
                return
            end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
                speedKeybinds[targetKey] = nil
            else
                for otherKeyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
                    if otherKeyId ~= targetKey and boundKey == input.KeyCode then
                        speedKeybinds[otherKeyId] = nil
                    end
                end
                if tpDownKeybind == input.KeyCode then
                    tpDownKeybind = nil
                    refreshTPDownKeybind()
                end
                speedKeybinds[targetKey] = input.KeyCode
            end
            listeningForSpeedKey = nil
            refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
            saveWaterHubConfig()
            return
        end
        if listeningForTPDownKey then
            if tick() - (keybindListenStartedAt or 0) < 0.18 then return end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Escape then
                listeningForTPDownKey = false
                refreshTPDownKeybind()
                return
            end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Backspace or input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Delete then
                tpDownKeybind = nil
            else
                for keyId, boundKey in pairs(speedKeybinds) do
                    if boundKey == input.KeyCode then
                        speedKeybinds[keyId] = nil
                    end
                end
                tpDownKeybind = input.KeyCode
            end
            listeningForTPDownKey = false
            refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
            refreshTPDownKeybind()
            saveWaterHubConfig()
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.SpeedToggle then
            toggleCarryMode()
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.LaggerToggle then
            toggleLaggerMode()
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.Aimbot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.Aimbot then
            if _G.SafeModeIsLocked and _G.SafeModeIsLocked() then
                if _G.SafeModeForceStop then _G.SafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
                return
            end
            if _G.AimbotToggle then
                _G.AimbotToggle()
            end
            if _G.AimbotSyncVisual then _G.AimbotSyncVisual() end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.TPBat and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.TPBat then
            if _G.SafeModeIsLocked and _G.SafeModeIsLocked() then
                if _G.SafeModeForceStop then _G.SafeModeForceStop("SAFE MODE LOCK") end
                return
            end
            if _G.TPBatToggle then
                _G.TPBatToggle()
            elseif _G.TPBatStart and _G.TPBatStop then
                if _G.TPBatOn then _G.TPBatStop() else _G.TPBatStart() end
            end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.DropBrainrot then
            runDropBrainrot()
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.AutoLeft and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AutoLeft then
            if _G.WaterHubSetAutoLeft then _G.WaterHubSetAutoLeft(not autoLeftEnabled) end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.AutoRight and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AutoRight then
            if _G.WaterHubSetAutoRight then _G.WaterHubSetAutoRight(not autoRightEnabled) end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.LaggerActivate and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.LaggerActivate then
            if toggleLagger then
                toggleLagger()
                if showActionNotification then showActionNotification(laggerEnabled and "LAGGER ON" or "LAGGER OFF") end
            end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.AntiDie and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.AntiDie then
            if _G.WaterHubSetAntiDie then
                _G.WaterHubSetAntiDie(not antiDieEnabled)
                if showActionNotification then showActionNotification(antiDieEnabled and "ANTI DIE ON" or "ANTI DIE OFF") end
            end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.InstantReset and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.InstantReset then
            if _G.InstantReset then _G.InstantReset() end
            return
        end
        if speedKeybinds.WaterHubLagger and input.KeyCode == speedKeybinds.WaterHubLagger then
            if _G.LaggerWindowToggle then _G.LaggerWindowToggle() end
            return
        end
        if tpDownKeybind and input.KeyCode == tpDownKeybind then
            runTPFloor()
            return
        end
    end)
end

            if not listening or gp then return end
            if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Unknown then return end
            speedKeybinds.LaggerActivate = input.KeyCode
            bindBtn.Text = keyName(input.KeyCode)
            listening = false
            glide(bindBtn, {BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.well})
            refreshAllSpeedKeybinds()
            saveWaterHubConfig()
        end)
        _G.LaggerWindowToggle = function()
            Panel.Visible = not Panel.Visible
            laggerWindowOpen = Panel.Visible
        end
    end
end

        local activeBody = {}
        local function bodyTag(name)
            return "WaterHubBody_" .. tostring(name):gsub("%s+", "")
        end
        local function attachBodyMesh(config)
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return false, "No character" end
            local target = char:FindFirstChild(config.targetBodyPart)
            if not target then return false, "Part not found" end
            local tag = bodyTag(config.name)
            local old = char:FindFirstChild(tag)
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            if config.partsToHide then
                for _, partName in ipairs(config.partsToHide) do
                    local limb = char:FindFirstChild(partName)
                    if limb and limb:IsA("BasePart") then limb.Transparency = 1 end
                end
            end
            if type(game.GetObjects) ~= "function" then return false, "Executor cannot load assets" end
            local ok, objects = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects(config.id) end)
            if not ok or not objects or #objects == 0 then return false, "Asset fetch failed" end
            local model = objects[1]
            model.Name = tag
            local mesh = model:IsA("BasePart") and model or model:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if not mesh then return false, "No mesh in asset" end
            mesh.CanCollide = false
            mesh.Anchored = false
            mesh.CFrame = target.CFrame
            local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            weld.Part0 = target
            weld.Part1 = mesh
            weld.Parent = mesh
            model.Parent = char
            if config.audio then
                local sound = Instance.new("Sound")
                sound.SoundId = config.audio
                sound.Volume = 0.5
                sound.Parent = mesh
                sound:Play()
                game:GetService("Debris"):AddItem(sound, 3)
            end
            return true
        end
        local function attachAccessory(config)
            local char = LP.Character
            if not char then return false, "No character" end
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if not hum then return false, "No humanoid" end
            if type(game.GetObjects) ~= "function" then return false, "Executor cannot load assets" end
            local ok, objects = pcall(function() return game:GetObjects(config.id) end)
            if not ok or not objects or #objects == 0 then return false, "Asset fetch failed" end
            local acc = objects[1]
            if not acc:IsA("Accessory") then
                acc = acc:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Accessory", true) or acc
            end
            local tag = bodyTag(config.name)
            local old = char:FindFirstChild(tag)
            if old then old:Destroy() end
            if acc:IsA("Accessory") then
                acc.Name = tag
                local handle = acc:FindFirstChild("Handle")
                if handle then handle.CanCollide = false end
                local added = pcall(function() hum:AddAccessory(acc) end)
                if not added then
                    acc.Parent = char
                end
                return true
            end
            local mesh = acc:IsA("BasePart") and acc or acc:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
            if not mesh then return false, "No accessory in asset" end
            local anchorPart = char:FindFirstChild(config.targetBodyPart or "Head")
            if not anchorPart then return false, "Part not found" end
            acc.Name = tag
            mesh.CanCollide = false
            mesh.Anchored = false
            mesh.CFrame = anchorPart.CFrame
            local weld = Instance.new("WeldConstraint")
            weld.Part0 = anchorPart
            weld.Part1 = mesh
            weld.Parent = mesh
            acc.Parent = char
            return true
        end
        local function applyBody(config)
            local ok, err
            if config.kind == "accessory" then
                ok, err = attachAccessory(config)
            else
                ok, err = attachBodyMesh(config)
            end
            if ok then activeBody[config.name] = config end
            return ok, err

            return tostring(value or ""):gsub("%D", "")

        local function buildCustomPack()
            local pack = {}
            local count = 0
            local idleId = digits(customAnimIds.idle)
            if idleId ~= "" then
                pack.idle = {{"rbxassetid://" .. idleId, 1}, {"rbxassetid://" .. idleId, 1}}
                count = count + 1
            end
            for _, slot in ipairs(ANIM_SLOTS) do
                if slot.key ~= "idle" then
                    local raw = digits(customAnimIds[slot.key])
                    if raw ~= "" then
                        pack[slot.key] = "rbxassetid://" .. raw
                        count = count + 1
                    end
                end
            end
            return pack, count
        end
        function _G.ApplyCustomAnimPack()
            local pack, count = buildCustomPack()
            if count == 0 then return false, "No animation ids" end
            AnimationPacks["Custom"] = pack
            local listed = false
            for _, name in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
                if name == "Custom" then listed = true break end
            end
            if not listed then table.insert(AnimationPackList, "Custom") end
            selectedAnimationPack = "Custom"
            for index, name in ipairs(AnimationPackList) do
                if name == "Custom" then AnimationPackIndex = index break end
            end
            if refreshAnimationPackRow then refreshAnimationPackRow() end
            applyAnimationPack("Custom")
            pcall(saveWaterHubConfig)
            return true, count
        end

do
    do
        local BIND_ALIASES = {
            SpeedToggle = "CarrySpeed",
            CarrySpeed = "CarrySpeed",
            LaggerToggle = "LaggerMode",
            LaggerMode = "LaggerMode",
            DropBrainrot = "DropLoot",
            DropLoot = "DropLoot",

            AntiDesyncAimbot = "TPBat",
            TPBat = "TPBat",
            AutoLeft = "SideLeft",
            SideLeft = "SideLeft",
            AutoRight = "SideRight",
            SideRight = "SideRight",
            InstantReset = "QuickReset",
            QuickReset = "QuickReset",
            ToggleUI = "PanelToggle",
            PanelToggle = "PanelToggle",
            WaterHubLagger = "NetChoke",
            NetChoke = "NetChoke",
            LaggerActivate = "LaggerActivate",
            AntiDie = "AntiDie",
        }
        local NUMBER_KEYS = {
            NS = "NS", CS = "CS",
            LAGGER_SPEED = "LAGGER_SPEED", LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = "LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED",
            AIMBOT_SPEED = "AIMBOT_SPEED", LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = "LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED",
            TPBAT_SPEED = "TPBAT_SPEED", TPBAT_SPEED = "TPBAT_SPEED",
            autoTPHeight = "autoTPHeight", fovValue = "fovValue",
            autoStealRadius = "autoStealRadius",
        }
        local FLAG_KEYS = {
            "autoCarrySpeedEnabled", "autoTPEnabled", "infJumpEnabled", "antiRagdollEnabled",
            "autoStealEnabled", "autoSwingEnabled", "mirrorTPDownEnabled",
            "batCounterEnabled", "medCounterEnabled", "autoResetOnMedEnabled",
            "autoInstaResetOnDeathEnabled", "espEnabled", "showTracerEnabled",
            "ragdollCountdownEnabled", "nukeOptimiserEnabled", "fovEnabled",
            "noCamCollisionEnabled", "autoLeftEnabled", "autoRightEnabled",
            "antiDieEnabled",
        }
        local function isConfigTable(data)
            if type(data) ~= "table" then return false, 0 end
            local score = 0
            if type(data.keybinds) == "table" then
                for key in pairs(data.keybinds) do
                    if BIND_ALIASES[key] then score = score + 1 end
                end
            end
            if data.tpDownKeybind ~= nil then score = score + 1 end
            for key in pairs(NUMBER_KEYS) do
                if tonumber(data[key]) then score = score + 1 end
            end
            for _, key in ipairs(FLAG_KEYS) do
                if type(data[key]) == "boolean" then score = score + 1 end
            end
            if type(data.accentColor) == "table" then score = score + 1 end
            if type(data.skyTheme) == "string" then score = score + 1 end
            return score > 0, score
        end
        local function readJson(path)
            if type(readfile) ~= "function" then return nil end
            local ok, body = pcall(readfile, path)
            if not ok or type(body) ~= "string" or body == "" then return nil end
            if not body:find("{", 1, true) then return nil end
            local decoded
            local fine = pcall(function() decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
            if not fine then return nil end
            return decoded
        end
        local function scanConfigs()
            local found = {}
            if type(listfiles) ~= "function" then return found end
            local seen = {}
            local function walk(dir, depth)
                if depth > 2 then return end
                local ok, items = pcall(listfiles, dir)
                if not ok or type(items) ~= "table" then return end
                for _, path in ipairs(items) do
                    if not seen[path] then
                        seen[path] = true
                        local folder = type(isfolder) == "function" and isfolder(path)
                        if folder then
                            walk(path, depth + 1)
                        else
                            local lower = path:lower()
                            if lower:find("%.json$") or lower:find("config") then
                                local data = readJson(path)
                                if data then
                                    local valid, score = isConfigTable(data)
                                    if valid then
                                        table.insert(found, {path = path, data = data, score = score})
                                    end
                                end
                            end
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            walk("", 0)
            table.sort(found, function(a, b) return a.score > b.score end)
            return found
        end
        local function importConfig(data)
            local applied = 0
            if type(data.keybinds) == "table" then
                for rawKey, value in pairs(data.keybinds) do
                    local target = BIND_ALIASES[rawKey]
                    if target and type(value) == "string" and value ~= "" and value ~= "None" then
                        local code = Enum.KeyCode[value]
                        if code then
                            speedKeybinds[target] = code
                            applied = applied + 1
                        end
                    end
                end
            end
            if type(data.tpDownKeybind) == "string" and data.tpDownKeybind ~= "None" then
                local code = Enum.KeyCode[data.tpDownKeybind]
                if code then
                    tpDownKeybind = code
                    applied = applied + 1
                end
            end
            for rawKey, target in pairs(NUMBER_KEYS) do
                local value = tonumber(data[rawKey])
                if value then
                    if target == "NS" then NS = value
                    elseif target == "CS" then CS = value
                    elseif target == "LAGGER_SPEED" then LAGGER_SPEED = value
                    elseif target == "LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED" then LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED = value
                    elseif target == "AIMBOT_SPEED" then AIMBOT_SPEED = value
                    elseif target == "LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED" then LAGGER_AIMBOT_SPEED = value
                    elseif target == "TPBAT_SPEED" then TPBAT_SPEED = value
                    elseif target == "autoTPHeight" then autoTPHeight = value
                    elseif target == "fovValue" then fovValue = value
                    elseif target == "autoStealRadius" then
                        if value == 7 or value == 8 or value == 9 then autoStealRadius = value end
                    end
                    applied = applied + 1
                end
            end
            for _, key in ipairs(FLAG_KEYS) do
                if type(data[key]) == "boolean" then
                    if key == "autoCarrySpeedEnabled" then autoCarrySpeedEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoTPEnabled" then autoTPEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "infJumpEnabled" then infJumpEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "antiRagdollEnabled" then antiRagdollEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoStealEnabled" then autoStealEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoSwingEnabled" then autoSwingEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "mirrorTPDownEnabled" then mirrorTPDownEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "batCounterEnabled" then batCounterEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "medCounterEnabled" then medCounterEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoResetOnMedEnabled" then autoResetOnMedEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoInstaResetOnDeathEnabled" then autoInstaResetOnDeathEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "espEnabled" then espEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "showTracerEnabled" then showTracerEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "ragdollCountdownEnabled" then ragdollCountdownEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "nukeOptimiserEnabled" then nukeOptimiserEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "fovEnabled" then fovEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "noCamCollisionEnabled" then noCamCollisionEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoLeftEnabled" then autoLeftEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "autoRightEnabled" then autoRightEnabled = data[key]
                    elseif key == "antiDieEnabled" then antiDieEnabled = data[key]
                    end
                    applied = applied + 1
                end
            end
            if type(data.skyTheme) == "string" and data.skyTheme ~= "" then
                skyTheme = data.skyTheme
                applied = applied + 1
            end
            if type(data.accentColor) == "table" then
                local c = tableToAccent(data.accentColor)
                if c then
                    applyAccent(c, true)
                    applied = applied + 1
                end
            end
            pcall(function()
                if normalSpeedBox then normalSpeedBox.Text = tostring(NS) end
                if carrySpeedBox then carrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(CS) end
                if laggerSpeedBox then laggerSpeedBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_SPEED) end
                if laggerCarrySpeedBox then laggerCarrySpeedBox.Text = tostring(LAGGER_CARRY_SPEED) end
                if autoTPHeightBox then autoTPHeightBox.Text = tostring(autoTPHeight) end
                if _G.WaterHubSetGrabRadius then _G.WaterHubSetGrabRadius(tostring(autoStealRadius)) end
                if _G.AimbotSyncSpeedBoxes then _G.AimbotSyncSpeedBoxes() end
                if refreshAllSpeedKeybinds then refreshAllSpeedKeybinds() end
                if refreshTPDownKeybind then refreshTPDownKeybind() end
                if _G.WaterHubApplySavedGameplayStates then _G.WaterHubApplySavedGameplayStates() end
                if _G.WaterHubSyncToggleVisuals then _G.WaterHubSyncToggleVisuals() end
            end)
            pcall(saveWaterHubConfig)
            return applied
        end
        local Panel = Instance.new("Frame")
        Panel.Name = "WaterHubImportWindow"
        Panel.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.void
        Panel.BorderSizePixel = 0
        Panel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 320, 0, 340)
        Panel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -160, 0.5, -170)
        Panel.Active = true
        Panel.ClipsDescendants = true
        Panel.Visible = false
        Panel.ZIndex = 20
        Panel.Parent = Gui
        round(Panel, 10)
        edge(Panel, SKIN.seam, 1, 0.3)
        grip(Panel)
        local art = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
        art.Name = "Art"
        art.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        art.BorderSizePixel = 0
        art.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        art.Image = ART_ASSET
        art.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Crop
        art.ImageTransparency = 0.2
        art.ZIndex = 20
        art.Parent = Panel
        local scrim = Instance.new("Frame")
        scrim.Name = "Scrim"
        scrim.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(5, 12, 28)
        scrim.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
        scrim.BorderSizePixel = 0
        scrim.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
        scrim.ZIndex = 20
        scrim.Parent = Panel
        local pip = Instance.new("Frame")
        pip.Name = "Pip"
        pip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 24)
        pip.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 14)
        pip.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT
        pip.BorderSizePixel = 0
        pip.ZIndex = 23
        pip.Parent = Panel
        round(pip, 2)
        registerAccent(function(c) pip.BackgroundColor3 = c end)
        local title = caption(Panel, "IMPORT", 15, TYPE_HEAVY, SKIN.ink)
        title.Name = "Title"
        title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 18)
        title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 14)
        title.ZIndex = 23
        local sub = caption(Panel, "CONFIG SCANNER", 8, TYPE_BOLD, SKIN.inkFaint)
        sub.Name = "Sub"
        sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 10)
        sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 23, 0, 31)
        sub.ZIndex = 23
        local close = chipButton(Panel, "\226\128\147", 26, 22)
        close.Name = "Close"
        close.Position = UDim2.new(1, -38, 0, 15)
        close.TextSize = 15
        close.ZIndex = 24
        close.Activated:Connect(function() Panel.Visible = false end)
        local rescan = chipButton(Panel, "SCAN", 54, 22)
        rescan.Name = "Rescan"
        rescan.Position = UDim2.new(1, -98, 0, 15)
        rescan.TextSize = 9
        rescan.ZIndex = 24
        local rule = Instance.new("Frame")
        rule.Name = "HeaderRule"
        rule.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.seam
        rule.BackgroundTransparency = 0.45
        rule.BorderSizePixel = 0
        rule.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 1)
        rule.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 50)

        local board = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
        board.Name = "Board"
        board.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        board.BorderSizePixel = 0
        board.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 60)
        board.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 1, -72)
        board.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
        board.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
        board.ScrollBarThickness = 2
        board.ScrollBarImageColor3 = SKIN.seam
        board.ScrollBarImageTransparency = 0.4

        boardLayout.Padding = UDim.new(0, 5)

        boardLayout.Parent = board
        inset(board, 0, 6, 0, 8)
        local function baseName(path)
            return tostring(path):match("([^/\\]+)$") or tostring(path)
        end
        local function clearBoard()
            for _, child in ipairs(board:GetChildren()) do
                if child:IsA("Frame") or child:IsA("TextLabel") then child:Destroy() end
            end
        end
        local function notice(text)
            local lbl = caption(board, text, 10, TYPE_BODY, SKIN.inkMute)
            lbl.Name = "Notice"
            lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 32)
            lbl.TextWrapped = true
            lbl.LayoutOrder = 1
            lbl.ZIndex = 22
        end
        local function entryRow(entry, index)
            local row = Instance.new("Frame")
            row.Name = "Cfg" .. index
            row.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.slab
            row.BackgroundTransparency = 0.2
            row.BorderSizePixel = 0
            row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 46)
            row.LayoutOrder = index
            row.ZIndex = 22
            row.Parent = board
            round(row, 7)
            local rowEdge = edge(row, SKIN.seamSoft, 1, 0.45)
            local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
            bar.Name = "Bar"
            bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
            bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0.5, 0)
            bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 20)
            bar.BackgroundColor3 = ACCENT
            bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
            bar.ZIndex = 23
            bar.Parent = row
            round(bar, 2)
            local name = caption(row, baseName(entry.path), 11, TYPE_BOLD, SKIN.ink)
            name.Name = "Label"
            name.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 14)
            name.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 8)
            name.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            name.ZIndex = 23
            local meta = caption(row, entry.score .. " settings found", 9, TYPE_BODY, SKIN.inkFaint)
            meta.Name = "Meta"
            meta.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 12)
            meta.Position = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 24)
            meta.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
            meta.ZIndex = 23
            local action = Instance.new("TextButton")
            action.Name = "Import"
            action.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
            action.BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.well
            action.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
            action.BorderSizePixel = 0
            action.AutoButtonColor = false
            action.Text = "IMPORT"
            action.TextColor3 = SKIN.ink
            action.TextSize = 9
            action.Font = TYPE_HEAVY
            action.Size = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 26)
            action.Position = UDim2.new(1, -10, 0.5, 0)
            action.ZIndex = 24
            action.Parent = row
            round(action, 5)
            local actionEdge = edge(action, SKIN.seam, 1, 0.3)
            action.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local count = importConfig(entry.data)
                action.Text = count > 0 and "DONE" or "EMPTY"
                meta.Text = count .. " settings imported"
                rowEdge.Color = ACCENT
                glide(rowEdge, {Transparency = 0.2})
                showActionNotification(count > 0 and ("IMPORTED " .. count .. " SETTINGS") or "NOTHING TO IMPORT")
                task.delay(2.5, function()
                    if not row.Parent then return end
                    action.Text = "IMPORT"
                    rowEdge.Color = SKIN.seamSoft
                    glide(rowEdge, {Transparency = 0.45})
                end)
            end)
            action.MouseEnter:Connect(function() glide(action, {BackgroundColor3 = accentDim(ACCENT, 0.34)}) end)
            action.MouseLeave:Connect(function() glide(action, {BackgroundColor3 = SKIN.well}) end)
        end
        local function refreshList()
            clearBoard()
            if type(listfiles) ~= "function" then
                notice("Executor has no file access, so configs cannot be scanned.")
                return
            end
            local found = scanConfigs()
            if #found == 0 then
                notice("No config files found. Drop them in your executor workspace folder and scan again.")
                return
            end
            for index, entry in ipairs(found) do
                entryRow(entry, index)
            end
        end
        rescan.Activated:Connect(refreshList)
        _G.ImportPanelToggle = function()
            Panel.Visible = not Panel.Visible
            if Panel.Visible then refreshList() end
        end
    end
